- **arc.es**: Cómo funciona ARCO (ARC, alias A) de AutoCAD en español, solicitud a solicitud: tres puntos, inicio-centro-fin o centro-inicio-fin, y Ctrl. Pruébalo gratis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/arc.es.md))

- **arc.ja**: AutoCAD の円弧(ARC、A)の描き方をプロンプト順に解説します。3 点、始点・中心・終点、中心・始点・終点、[Ctrl]で時計回り。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/arc.ja.md))
- **arc.ko**: AutoCAD 호(ARC, A) 그리기를 프롬프트 순서대로 설명합니다: 3점, 시작점-중심-끝점, 중심-시작점-끝점, Ctrl 키로 시계 방향. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/arc.ko.md))
- **arc.pl**: Jak narysować łuk w AutoCADzie poleceniem ŁUK (ARC, U): przez 3 punkty, początek-środek-koniec lub środek-początek-koniec, z Ctrl zgodnie ze wskazówkami zegara. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/arc.pl.md))
- **arc.pt-BR**: Como funciona o ARC do AutoCAD em português, ARCO (alias A), prompt a prompt: três pontos, início-centro-fim ou centro-início-fim, e o Ctrl. Experimente grátis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/arc.pt-BR.md))
- **arc.ru**: Как начертить дугу в AutoCAD командой ДУГА (ARC, Д): по трем точкам, начало-центр-конец или центр-начало-конец, и по часовой стрелке с Ctrl. Попробуйте онлайн. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/arc.ru.md))

- **arcgis-to-portaljs**: Migrate a whole ArcGIS Hub site (opendata.arcgis.com or a Hub Premium custom domain) into a PortalJS Arc portal end-to-end. Harvests the Hub /data.json (DCAT-US) inventory, exports every FeatureService layer through the ArcGIS REST query API (resultOffset paging), converts each to the serverless dual tier (PMTiles render + GeoParquet query) with tabular items to Parquet, pushes everything to Cloudflare R2 via Git LFS, appends dual-tier datasets.json entries, and writes a source-vs-derived parity report. The reusable arcgis-to-portaljs migrator. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/arcgis-to-portaljs.md) · ★2.4k)
